Use to review whether the degraded values and cached results a policy returns are SAFE and CORRECT. REJECT by default. - **review-r8e-policy-observability**: Adversarial review of an r8e policy's observability and operability — whether the policy is NAMED (registered for health) and, when built per job or request, removed from its registry, whether WithHooks is wired to the failure modes it actually has and is safe for concurrent use, whether the breaker's neutral outcomes are visible, the READINESS IMPACT (and the fleet-wide flip risk of gating a shared dependency), config-vs-code & hot-reload reach, OTel wiring and error codes, and the chaos kill-switch. Use to review whether a policy can be SEEN and OPERATED in production. REJECT by default. ([byte4ever/r8e](https://github.com/byte4ever/r8e/tree/HEAD/policy-skills/review-r8e-policy-observability))
- **review-r8e-policy-overload**: Adversarial review of the overload / shedding controls of an r8e policy — circuit breaker, bulkhead (own or shared), adaptive concurrency, rate limit, cooldown, throttle, SLO governor. Judges presence and sizing of these guards against the downstream's capacity and sharing, the brownout (slow-but-200) trip, what the breaker counts (outcome classifier, local rejections), half-open probes that never return (probe lease), healing slow-start, the bulkhead/adaptive-concurrency and shared-guard mutual exclusions, and metastable collapse from retry/hedge amplification on a shared dependency with not… ([byte4ever/r8e](https://github.com/byte4ever/r8e/tree/HEAD/policy-skills/review-r8e-policy-overload))
